Our Leadership
"At Sahrdaya, we believe in nurturing not just engineers, but responsible citizens who contribute meaningfully to society. Our commitment to academic excellence, moral values, and holistic development makes Sahrdaya a unique institution."
Fr. Anto Chungath
Executive Director
"Sahrdaya stands at the forefront of engineering education in Kerala, consistently striving for innovation in teaching, research, and student development. We are dedicated to shaping the leaders of tomorrow."
Dr. Ramkumar S
Principal

Industry Partners
Industry Advisory Board
Sahrdaya has constituted an Industry Advisory Board with distinguished professionals and leaders from top companies to guide curriculum design, research initiatives, and placement strategies.
Training & Placement Cell
Enjoy, Learn and Live in This Fab Campus
Athletics & Fitness
Sahrdaya has excellent sports and recreation facilities on campus including basketball, football, cricket grounds, gym, and indoor sports arena.
Housing & Dining
Sahrdaya provides comfortable housing options for students with separate hostels for boys and girls, 24/7 security, and quality dining facilities.
Art & Culture
Nurturing a rich tradition of creativity, culture, drama, music, and dance through various clubs, annual festivals, and cultural programs.
Ready to Shape Your Future?
Join Sahrdaya College of Engineering & Technology and be part of a vibrant community that nurtures innovation and excellence.

Sahrdaya College of Engineering & Technology - Leading Engineering Institution Kerala
Sahrdaya College of Engineering and Technology (SCET) has achieved remarkable recognition as one of the top 15 engineering colleges in Kerala, moving up from rank 24 to rank 15 in 2025. This autonomous engineering college maintains NAAC B+ accreditation and NBA accreditation.
Located in Kodakara, Thrissur, Kerala, with 1800+ students, 180+ faculty, 90%+ placement rate. Programs: Computer Science Engineering, Electronics Engineering, Biotechnology, Biomedical Engineering, AI & ML, Data Science, Cyber Security. Affiliated to APJ Abdul Kalam Technological University. Best engineering college Thrissur, Kerala autonomous KEAM GATE admission 2026.

=== URL: https://www.sahrdaya.ac.in/announcements ===

Title: Sahrdaya College of Engineering & Technology | Top 15 Engineering College in Kerala | Autonomous Institution

Description: Now Ranked #15! One of the Best Engineering Colleges in Kerala. NAAC B+ Accredited | NBA Accredited Programs | 1800+ Students | 90%+ Placement Rate | Affiliated to APJ Abdul Kalam Technological University | Best Engineering College in Thrissur, Kerala

Announcements
Official announcements and notices
Upskill, Teachers training program
3 days ago
The CSE Department of Sahrdaya College of Engineering & Technology is organizing a three-day faculty development program titled “UPSKILL: AI Training for School Teachers 2026” on April 21, 22, and 23, from 9:30 AM to 4:00 PM. This hands-on training aims to equip school teachers with practical exposure to modern AI tools and their applications in education. Led by certified Intel AI experts, the program focuses on enhancing teaching methodologies through technology integration. The sessions will be conducted at the Multi media hall, and participants will receive certificates upon successful completion.
Inauguration of the Upgraded High-Speed Campus Wi-Fi Network
3 days ago
We are pleased to announce that the official inauguration of our college’s newly upgraded high-speed Wi-Fi network will be held on Monday, 20th April 2026 at 4:00 PM at the Administrative Block.
This significant upgrade marks a major step towards enhancing digital connectivity across the campus, ensuring seamless internet access to support academic excellence, research, and innovation.
The inauguration ceremony will be graced by our respected Manager, Msgr. Wilson Erathara, who will formally launch the upgraded network.
Thinkable Orientation Session
4 weeks ago
Step into Thinkable Orientation Session and discover how your ideas can evolve into assistive solutions that create real social impact, gain insights into the Assistive Innovation Hackathon, Thinkable – Where Ideas Enable Lives, conducted by Sahrdaya College of Engineering and Technology under STRIDE Initiative
Session by:
Mr. Robin Francis
Junior Program Executive, K-DISC
Workshop on Next generation Sequencing Data Analysis
1 month ago
The Department of Biotechnology at Sahrdaya College of Engineering & Technology is organizing a two-day workshop on Next Generation Sequencing (NGS) Data Analysis on 25th and 26th March 2026 at the MB Computer Centre. The session will be led by Mr. Ravisankar V, Senior Information Officer at Kerala Agricultural University, who brings extensive expertise in bioinformatics and data analysis. This workshop is designed to provide participants with hands-on experience and practical insights into NGS data processing, analysis pipelines, and interpretation of genomic data. Aimed at S6 Biotechnology students, the event will enhance their understanding of modern sequencing technologies and equip them with essential computational skills relevant to research and industry.
Data Science Club Inaguration
1 month ago
The Data Pulse – Data Science Club of Sahrdaya College of Engineering and Technology is being inaugurated to create a vibrant platform for students to explore the rapidly evolving field of data science and artificial intelligence. The club aims to foster innovation, analytical thinking, and practical learning through workshops, expert talks, competitions, and collaborative projects.
The inauguration ceremony will feature an invited talk by Prof. Dr. Sabu M K, Professor, Department of Computer Applications, Cochin University of Science and Technology, on the topic “Retrieval Augmented Generation: Enhancing LLMs with External Data.” The session will provide valuable insights into emerging AI technologies and their real-world applications.
Logo Design Competition
1 month ago
​🚀 Calling all Designers! 🚀
​The Data Science Club is hosting a Digital Logo Design Competition! We invite students from Semesters 2, 4, and 6 to showcase their creativity.
​The Challenge: Design a logo reflecting intelligence, innovation, and technology.
​The Prize: The winning design becomes our Official Logo, and the winner will be awarded!
​Show us your vision for the future of Data Science!
Session on REGULATORY COMPLIANCE FOR MEDICAL DEVICES: FDA, ISO, IEC, AND ICMED STANDARDS
1 month ago
Department of Biomedical Engineering in association with NDLI and EBSA is conducting a session on REGULATORY COMPLIANCE FOR MEDICAL DEVICES: FDA, ISO, IEC, AND ICMED STANDARDS by Ms. Mary Roopsy Joy (Alumni 2002 Batch), Quality Assurance and Regulatory Affairs Expert for Medical Devices on 9th March 2026.
Becoming Her
1 month ago
The Women’s Innovation Club of Innovation and Entrepreneurship Development Centre at Sahrdaya College of Engineering & Technology presents “Becoming Her”, a three-day online interactive talk series as part of Women’s Day celebrations.
The sessions explore inspiring female characters and the qualities that shape confidence, resilience, and leadership.
Herwall
1 month ago
As part of the Women’s Day celebrations, the Women Innovation Club invites students to participate in Her Wall—a space to celebrate the women who inspire us. Paste a picture of a woman who has influenced your life and be part of honoring their impact.
She speaks 2.0
1 month ago
She Speaks 2.0 is a debate competition organized as part of the Women’s Day celebrations, providing a platform for students to express their ideas, debate on thought-provoking topics, and showcase their speaking skills.
🔋 Smarter Energy for Modern Living  ( A Talk on Energy Management & Today’s Energy Scenario )
1 month ago
📢 IEEE Control Systems Society – Sahrdaya SB presents
🔋 Smarter Energy for Modern Living
( A Talk on Energy Management & Today’s Energy Scenario )
📅 5 March 2026 (Thursday)
⏰ 10:00 AM – 12:30 PM
🎓 For 2nd & 3rd Year EEE Students
The IEEE Control Systems Society – Sahrdaya Student Branch, in association with the Department of Electrical and Electronics Engineering, is organizing an insightful technical talk titled “Smarter Energy for Modern Living” on 5th March 2026, exclusively for Second and Third Year EEE students.
This session focuses on one of the most significant domains in modern electrical engineering — Energy Management and the Present Energy Scenario at both national and global levels.
About the Speaker
Adil Nasser
Assistant Professor, College of Engineering Trikaripur
State Coordinator, APJ KTU NSS Energy Cell
Gain insights into the current energy scenario, renewable integration, and energy management practices from an expert in the field.

Voltx Workshop
2 months ago
"The Department of Electrical and Electronics Engineering, in association with the project team SCET VAIDYUTH, is conducting a workshop titled VOLT X from 4th February to 27th February 2026 for S2 students.
The workshop aims to provide basic-level technical training related to the development of a solar-powered autonomous electric vehicle. The training will include an introduction to the software tools used in the project, namely MATLAB and TinkerCAD, along with fundamental electrical concepts such as wiring practices, electrical components, types of motors, and their specifications. Students will also be introduced to the mechanical works and integrations involved in the project, giving them an overall understanding of how electrical, software, and mechanical systems are combined in a real-time application.
Registration Fee : 30 Rs/-
Registration link : https://forms.gle/fGjGobv49h4VWxET9
The faculty in charge for the workshop are Dr. V. Vijikala and Sreekanth S, Department of Electrical and Electronics Engineering."

Title: Sahrdaya College of Engineering & Technology | Top 15 Engineering College in Kerala | Autonomous Institution

Description: Now Ranked #15! One of the Best Engineering Colleges in Kerala. NAAC B+ Accredited | NBA Accredited Programs | 1800+ Students | 90%+ Placement Rate | Affiliated to APJ Abdul Kalam Technological University | Best Engineering College in Thrissur, Kerala

Now Hiring: Leadership & Faculty Positions
We are seeking dynamic professionals across all departments for the following roles:
I. Strategic Leadership Opportunities (Deans)
We seek transformative leaders for the following portfolios to drive our institutional vision:
Academic Excellence
Dean (UG) | Dean (PG)
Research & Innovation
Dean (Research) | Dean (Consultancy and Industry Connect)
Operations & Growth
Dean (Administration) | Dean (Planning)
Student Success
Dean (Student Affairs) | Dean (Career Guidance, Training, and Placement)
II. Faculty Positions
Senior Professor | Professor | Associate Professor | Assistant Professor
Departments & Areas of Specialization
Department
Areas of Specialization & Research
Industry & Consultancy Verticals
AI / ML & Computer Science
Generative & Agentic AI: LLMs, RAG, Transformers, Few-shot learning. Future Tech: Quantum ML/Cryptography, Edge Intelligence, Human-Computer Interaction (HCI), Affective Computing.
AI Ethics auditing, MLOps, Data Engineering, UI/UX Design, and AI-integrated Full-stack development.
Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS)
Smart Systems: Real-time systems (RTOS), IoT/IIoT, Sensor Fusion, LPWAN (5G/6G), and Digital Twins. Security: Cyber Forensics, Zero-trust Network Security.
Industrial Automation (Industry 4.0), Smart City infrastructure, and Secure Network protocols.
Electronics
Silicon Engineering: Advanced VLSI/ASIC Design, FPGA-based Systems, Signal & Image Processing, Low-power IC Design for Wearables.
Hardware Security, SoC (System on Chip) prototyping, and Hardware-level encryption services.
Biotechnology
Modern Bio-Eng: Computational/Systems Biology, Synthetic Biology, Biopharmaceuticals, Bioprocess Engineering.
Bio-process optimization, industrial fermentation, and bio-fuel research.
Biomedical Engineering
Med-Tech: Biosensor fabrication, Biomechanics, Physiological modelling, Virtual Instrumentation (LabVIEW/Simulink).
CAD/Prototyping of medical devices, diagnostic tool design, and regulatory compliance.
Electrical & Computer Eng.
Energy Systems: EV Charging Infrastructure, Battery Management (BMS), Renewable Energy Integration, AI-driven Control.
Microgrid management, Energy Auditing, and Smart Grid stabilization for industry.
Civil
Resilient Infra: Structural Health Monitoring (SHM), BIM (4D/5D Modeling), Revit architecture, GIS & Remote Sensing.
Green Building Certification (LEED), BIM-led Project Management, and Smart Materials.
Applied Science & Humanities
Foundational Research: Stochastic modeling for AI, Nanomaterials, Ethics in Technology, and Professional Communication.
Mathematical modeling for Finance, Material characterization, and Tech-Societal impact studies.
The Sahrdaya Value Proposition: A Path of Growth and Fulfillment
A Culture of Innovation
:
Active involvement in centres of excellence, product development, and industry-academia collaboration.
Scholar-Entrepreneur Pathway
:
Opportunities for funded projects, patent generation, and consultancy.
Interdisciplinary Synergy
:
A collaborative environment where faculty work on collaborative teaching, integrated projects and joint research.
Holistic Mentorship
:
A platform to mentor high-achieving students for global competitions and higher studies.
Future-Ready Environment
:
Access to advanced laboratories and emerging AI technologies.
Candidate Profile
Qualifications:
Ph.D./M.Tech from leading and reputed institutions (IITs, NITs, or equivalent), and industry as per AICTE/UGC norms.
Expertise:
Proven track record in reputed journals, industry collaboration, and research.
Vision:
Ability to design curriculum and teach advanced courses in emerging allied areas.
How to Apply
Join us in redefining the engineering landscape. Please submit your comprehensive CV, a detailed Research Statement, and your vision for student “future-proofing” to
career2026@sahrdaya.ac.in

Student Clubs
Explore our vibrant student clubs that foster learning, innovation, and community service. Join us in making a difference and enhancing your college experience.
NSS
National Service Scheme
NSS is a voluntary association of students committed to social service and community development. Through various programs and initiatives, NSS volunteers work towards creating a positive impact in society.
Learn More
IEDC
Innovation and Entrepreneurship Development Centre
IEDC fosters innovation and entrepreneurship among students by providing mentorship, resources, and opportunities to transform ideas into successful ventures. We nurture the entrepreneurial spirit and promote innovation-driven initiatives.
Learn More
PALS
Peer Assisted Learning System
PALS is a collaborative learning initiative where senior students guide and mentor their juniors. This peer-to-peer learning approach enhances academic performance and builds a strong community of learners.
Learn More
Get Involved
Student clubs are an integral part of campus life at Sahrdaya College. They provide opportunities for personal growth, skill development, and making lasting friendships. Click on any club to learn more about their activities and how to join.

HOD Message
About Computer Science & Engineering
The Department of Computer Science and Engineering at Sahrdaya College of Engineering and Technology is committed to excellence in education, research, and service. We prepare students to become leaders and innovators in the rapidly evolving field of computer science and engineering.
Established 2009
NBA Accredited
400+ Students
20+ Faculty
Vision
To be a center of excellence in computer science and engineering education, fostering innovation, research, and ethical leadership to address global technological challenges and contribute to societal development.
Mission
To provide quality education in computer science and engineering through innovative teaching methodologies, cutting-edge research, industry collaboration, and comprehensive skill development to produce competent professionals and entrepreneurs.
Department Overview
The Computer Science and Engineering department offers comprehensive undergraduate and postgraduate programs designed to meet the evolving needs of the technology industry. Our curriculum combines theoretical foundations with practical applications, ensuring students are well-prepared for successful careers in software development, research, and entrepreneurship.
With state-of-the-art laboratories, experienced faculty, and strong industry partnerships, we provide an environment that encourages innovation, critical thinking, and professional growth. Our graduates have consistently demonstrated excellence in various fields including artificial intelligence, data science, cybersecurity, and software engineering.
Key Highlights
Academic Excellence
• NBA accredited programs
• Updated curriculum aligned with industry needs
• Outcome-based education approach
• Strong placement record
Research & Innovation
• Active research in emerging technologies
• Student research projects
• Industry collaboration
• Publications in reputed journals

Innovative TLM

Sahrdaya College of Engineering & Technology
Innovative Teaching Learning Methods
Creating an open, creative and trustworthy learning environment
The innovative teaching-learning process is designed to create an open, creative, and trustworthy space for students to grow, take risks, and feel comfortable in their patterns of learning. Faculty initiate key actions to foster a more entrepreneurial and innovation-driven classroom experience.
Our Approach
We combine traditional pedagogy with modern digital tools to deliver outcomes-based education aligned with industry needs and KTU guidelines.
Teaching Methods Employed
Digital Learning
Classroom Quiz
Virtual Labs
Video Lectures
Low-Stakes Testing
Concept Mapping
Roleplay
Modern Tool Usage
Miniature Scale Models
MOOCs
Concept Tests
Audio-Visual Aids
Collaborative Learning
Industry Mentorship
Activity-Based Learning
Buzz Groups
Pair Interaction
Alumni Interaction
Case Study
Mastery Learning
One-To-One Communication
Website-Based Learning

ECE
Innovative TLM
Electronics & Communication Engineering
Sahrdaya College of Engineering & Technology
Innovative Teaching Learning Methods
Creating an open, creative and trustworthy learning environment
The innovative teaching-learning process is designed to create an open, creative, and trustworthy space for students to grow, take risks, and feel comfortable in their patterns of learning. Faculty initiate key actions to foster a more entrepreneurial and innovation-driven classroom experience.
Our Approach
We combine traditional pedagogy with modern digital tools to deliver outcomes-based education aligned with industry needs and KTU guidelines.
Teaching Methods Employed
Digital Learning
Classroom Quiz
Virtual Labs
Video Lectures
Low-Stakes Testing
Concept Mapping
Roleplay
Modern Tool Usage
Miniature Scale Models
MOOCs
Concept Tests
Audio-Visual Aids
Collaborative Learning
Industry Mentorship
Activity-Based Learning
Buzz Groups
Pair Interaction
Alumni Interaction
Case Study
Mastery Learning
One-To-One Communication

Innovative TLM
Electrical & Electronics Engineering
Sahrdaya College of Engineering & Technology
Innovative Teaching Learning Methods
Creating an open, creative and trustworthy learning environment
The innovative teaching-learning process is designed to create an open, creative, and trustworthy space for students to grow, take risks, and feel comfortable in their patterns of learning. Faculty initiate key actions to foster a more entrepreneurial and innovation-driven classroom experience.
Our Approach
We combine traditional pedagogy with modern digital tools to deliver outcomes-based education aligned with industry needs and KTU guidelines.
Teaching Methods Employed
Digital Learning
Classroom Quiz
Virtual Labs
Video Lectures
Low-Stakes Testing
Concept Mapping
Roleplay
Modern Tool Usage
Miniature Scale Models
MOOCs
Concept Tests
Audio-Visual Aids
Collaborative Learning
Industry Mentorship
Activity-Based Learning
Buzz Groups
Pair Interaction
Alumni Interaction
Case Study
Mastery Learning
One-To-One Communication
Website-Based Learning

CE
Innovative TLM
Civil Engineering
Sahrdaya College of Engineering & Technology
Innovative Teaching Learning Methods
Creating an open, creative and trustworthy learning environment
The innovative teaching-learning process is designed to create an open, creative, and trustworthy space for students to grow, take risks, and feel comfortable in their patterns of learning. Faculty initiate key actions to foster a more entrepreneurial and innovation-driven classroom experience.
Our Approach
We combine traditional pedagogy with modern digital tools to deliver outcomes-based education aligned with industry needs and KTU guidelines.
Teaching Methods Employed
Digital Learning
Classroom Quiz
Virtual Labs
Video Lectures
Low-Stakes Testing
Concept Mapping
Roleplay
Modern Tool Usage
Miniature Scale Models
MOOCs
Concept Tests
Audio-Visual Aids
Collaborative Learning
Industry Mentorship
Activity-Based Learning
Buzz Groups
Pair Interaction
Alumni Interaction
Case Study
Mastery Learning
One-To-One Communication
Website-Based Learning

Funded Projects
Active research projects with external funding and institutional support
The department actively pursues externally funded research across domains including AI, computer vision, IoT, and blockchain technologies. Projects are funded by premier agencies such as DST-SERB, AICTE, and state missions.

BME
Innovative TLM
Biomedical Engineering
Sahrdaya College of Engineering & Technology
Innovative Teaching Learning Methods
Creating an open, creative and trustworthy learning environment
The innovative teaching-learning process is designed to create an open, creative, and trustworthy space for students to grow, take risks, and feel comfortable in their patterns of learning. Faculty initiate key actions to foster a more entrepreneurial and innovation-driven classroom experience.
Our Approach
We combine traditional pedagogy with modern digital tools to deliver outcomes-based education aligned with industry needs and KTU guidelines.
Teaching Methods Employed
Digital Learning
Classroom Quiz
Virtual Labs
Video Lectures
Low-Stakes Testing
Concept Mapping
Roleplay
Modern Tool Usage
Miniature Scale Models
MOOCs
Concept Tests
Audio-Visual Aids
Collaborative Learning
Industry Mentorship
Activity-Based Learning
Buzz Groups
Pair Interaction
Alumni Interaction
Case Study
Mastery Learning
One-To-One Communication
Website-Based Learning

Sahrdaya College of Engineering & Technology
Funded Projects
Active research projects with external funding and institutional support
The department actively pursues externally funded research across domains including AI, computer vision, IoT, and blockchain technologies. Projects are funded by premier agencies such as DST-SERB, AICTE, and state missions.

BTE
Innovative TLM
Biotechnology
Sahrdaya College of Engineering & Technology
Innovative Teaching Learning Methods
Creating an open, creative and trustworthy learning environment
The innovative teaching-learning process is designed to create an open, creative, and trustworthy space for students to grow, take risks, and feel comfortable in their patterns of learning. Faculty initiate key actions to foster a more entrepreneurial and innovation-driven classroom experience.
Our Approach
We combine traditional pedagogy with modern digital tools to deliver outcomes-based education aligned with industry needs and KTU guidelines.
Teaching Methods Employed
Digital Learning
Classroom Quiz
Virtual Labs
Video Lectures
Low-Stakes Testing
Concept Mapping
Roleplay
Modern Tool Usage
Miniature Scale Models
MOOCs
Concept Tests
Audio-Visual Aids
Collaborative Learning
Industry Mentorship
Activity-Based Learning
Buzz Groups
Pair Interaction
Alumni Interaction
Case Study
Mastery Learning
One-To-One Communication
Website-Based Learning

Funded Projects
Active research projects with external funding and institutional support
The department actively pursues externally funded research across domains including AI, computer vision, IoT, and blockchain technologies. Projects are funded by premier agencies such as DST-SERB, AICTE, and state missions.

AS&H
Innovative TLM
Applied Science & Humanities
Sahrdaya College of Engineering & Technology
Innovative Teaching Learning Methods
Creating an open, creative and trustworthy learning environment
The innovative teaching-learning process is designed to create an open, creative, and trustworthy space for students to grow, take risks, and feel comfortable in their patterns of learning. Faculty initiate key actions to foster a more entrepreneurial and innovation-driven classroom experience.
Our Approach
We combine traditional pedagogy with modern digital tools to deliver outcomes-based education aligned with industry needs and KTU guidelines.
Teaching Methods Employed
Digital Learning
Classroom Quiz
Virtual Labs
Video Lectures
Low-Stakes Testing
Concept Mapping
Roleplay
Modern Tool Usage
Miniature Scale Models
MOOCs
Concept Tests
Audio-Visual Aids
Collaborative Learning
Industry Mentorship
Activity-Based Learning
Buzz Groups
Pair Interaction
Alumni Interaction
Case Study
Mastery Learning
One-To-One Communication
Website-Based Learning
